Situation: The regiment is making its way down the scouted route. Divisional reconnaissance has stopped responding to calls, they are suspected to have been eliminated by unknown forces at the town ahead. You are the Forward Security Element and have been tasked with clearing out the enemy positions before the regiment arrives, so they can smoothly continue their movement forward without delay.\n\n\nEnemy: Company Strength, {{A}}x Leopard A{{B}}A{{C}}, {{D}}x Marder, {{E}}x Infantry Squads\n\n\nFriendly: {{F}}x T-{{G}}A, {{H}}x BMP-{{I}}, {{J}}x Infantry Squads. Reinforcements of {{K}}x T-{{L}}B, {{M}}x BMP-{{N}}, {{O}}x Infantry Squads. Support assets are {{P}}x HE Firemissions, {{Q}}x Smoke Missions and {{R}}x Illumination Missions, {{S}}x SU-{{T}} with FAB-{{U}} bombs\n\n\nMission: Find the enemy near the town, eliminate them and any potential enemy reinforcements before setting up forward security behind the town.\n\n\nCoordinating instructions: N/A\n\n\nEnd Conditions-\n-Red Victory {{V}}- {{W}}% Town Defence & QRF Destroyed. Limited Losses\n-Red Victory {{X}}- {{Y}}% Town Defence & QRF Destroyed. Severe Losses\n-Red Defeat- Loss of all player tanks (Including Mobility)\n=情况: 该团正沿着侦察路线行进。师级侦察部队已停止回应呼叫，怀疑他们已被前方城镇的未知力量消灭。你作为前沿警戒分队，任务是在团级主力到达前肃清敌方阵地，以便他们能顺利继续前进，不延误时间。\n\n敌军: 连级规模，{{A}}x 豹式 A{{B}}A{{C}}，{{D}}x 步兵战车，{{E}}x 步兵班\n\n友军: {{F}}x T-{{G}}A，{{H}}x BMP-{{I}}，{{J}}x 步兵班。增援力量包括 {{K}}x T-{{L}}B，{{M}}x BMP-{{N}}，{{O}}x 步兵班。支援力量有 {{P}}x 高爆弹开火任务，{{Q}}x 烟雾弹任务和 {{R}}x 照明弹任务，以及携带 FAB-{{U}} 炸弹的 {{S}}x SU-{{T}}。\n\n任务: 寻找城镇附近的敌军，消灭他们及任何潜在的敌方增援部队，随后在城镇后方建立前沿警戒。\n\n协调指示: 无\n\n结束条件-\n-红方胜利 {{V}}- 摧毁 {{W}}% 的城镇守军与快速反应部队。损失较小\n-红方胜利 {{X}}- 摧毁 {{Y}}% 的城镇守军与快速反应部队。损失惨重\n-红方失败- 所有玩家坦克损失（包括机动性丧失）\n

Your infantry will attack OBJ Ocelot once you have destroyed the enemy armor on OBJ Ocelot.\r\n\r\nEnd Conditions-\r\n-Blue Victory- {{Y}}% Enemy Destroyed and Objectives Cougar & Ocelot taken.\r\n-Blue Defeat- Loss of all player tanks\r\n=态势：您在大约{{A}}小时前阻止了敌军的攻击。您的营其余部队已经抵达，正在计划有限的反击。我方侦察兵观察到敌军正在构筑工事，如果再拖延攻击将更加困难。友军师属炮兵正在执行反炮兵火力任务，以压制敌军在此次攻击中的炮兵支援。\r\n\r\n敌军：连级力量，{{B}}辆T{{C}}A，{{D}}辆BTR{{E}}，{{F}}个步兵班。{{G}}辆T{{H}}预备部队\r\n\r\n友军：{{I}}辆Leo A{{J}}A{{K}}，{{L}}辆M{{M}}G，{{N}}个步兵班。增援部队{{O}}辆Leo A{{P}}A{{Q}}。支援火力为{{R}}次火力任务和{{S}}次WP烟幕任务。\r\n\r\n任务：使用您的{{T}}辆坦克，攻击目标美洲狮上的敌军，以支援第{{U}}机械化排的下车步兵攻击。一旦夺取目标美洲狮，第{{V}}坦克排和第{{W}}机械化排将前出支援您。然后您将前进攻击目标豹猫上的任何敌军，以支援第{{X}}排的下车步兵攻击。\r\n\r\n协调指令：一旦您与目标美洲狮上的敌军交战，您的步兵将发起攻击。一旦您摧毁目标豹猫上的敌方装甲，您的步兵将攻击目标豹猫。\r\n\r\n结束条件-\r\n-蓝方胜利-{{Y}}%敌军被摧毁且目标美洲狮和豹猫被夺取。\r\n-蓝方失败-所有玩家坦克损失\r\n
This is a simple mission to get used to the Leopard A{{A}}A{{B}} and its fire control. There will be {{C}} targets total: four of them static and {{D}} of them moving, all of them at different ranges.\r\n\r\n-A quick guide to using the fire control system\r\nPress Shift to enter the gunsight. Hold the right mouse button to move the gun. Center the gunsight on the target. Press E to enable the stereoscopic rangefinder. Press CTRL+Mouse Wheel (or PgUp and PgDown) and adjust the range until the target comes into focus. This will range your gun to the correct distance. Press E and use your normal gunsight to engage the enemy. Repeat these steps to kill all targets.\r\n\r\n-If you press C when looking through your gunsight, it will switch to your backup sight.\r\n-The night sight does not have the stereoscopic rangefinder.\r\n-The HUD shows your current range setting. Make sure you’re using a range that makes sense for the current target, especially if the coincidence rangefinder isn’t clear enough.\r\n\r\n-Simple Leopard A{{E}}A{{F}} ammunition description\r\nAPDS is a dart that flies fast and is good at killing tanks.\r\nHEAT is an explosive that flies slow and is good for killing vehicles lighter than tanks, as well as old tanks.\r\nHESH is an explosive that flies slow and is good for killing vehicles lighter than tanks, as well as old tanks.\r\n\r\nYou can see this briefing in the mission if you press M on your keyboard.\r\n=本任务旨在帮助您熟悉Leopard A{{A}}A{{B}}及其火控系统。总计有{{C}}个目标：4个固定目标和{{D}}个移动目标，均处于不同距离。\r\n\r\n-火控系统快速使用指南\r\n按Shift键进入炮手瞄准镜。按住鼠标右键移动火炮。将瞄准镜中心对准目标。按E键启用体视测距仪。按CTRL+鼠标滚轮（或PgUp和PgDown键）调整测距，直到目标图像清晰重合。这将为您的火炮标定正确距离。再按E键切换回主瞄准镜与敌交战。重复以上步骤，消灭所有目标。\r\n\r\n-在炮手瞄准镜视角下按C键，将切换至备用瞄准镜。\r\n-夜视瞄准镜没有体视测距功能。\r\n-抬头显示器（HUD）会显示您当前的测距设定。请确保该距离与当前目标相符，尤其是在合像式测距仪图像不够清晰时。\r\n\r\n-Leopard A{{E}}A{{F}}弹药简介\r\nAPDS是一种高速飞行的穿甲弹，擅于摧毁坦克。\r\nHEAT是一种低速飞行的爆炸物，适用于摧毁轻于坦克的载具及旧式坦克。\r\nHESH是一种低速飞行的爆炸物，适用于摧毁轻于坦克的载具及旧式坦克。\r\n\r\n任务期间，您可以随时按M键查看此简报。\r\n

This is a simple mission to get used to the Marder A{{A}}+ and its fire control. There will be {{B}} targets total: four of them static and {{C}} of them moving, all of them at different ranges.\r\n\r\n-A quick guide to using the fire control system\r\nPress Shift to enter the gunsight. Hold the right mouse button to move the gun. Press CTRL+Mouse Wheel (or PgUp and PgDown) to range the gun using the range number in the HUD. Center the gunsight on the target, and aim ahead of the target’s position if the target is moving. Fire on the target and adjust your range as needed. Repeat these steps to kill all targets.\r\n\r\nTo use the nightsight press T when looking through the gunsight. Then press N to turn on the IR searchlight. On the Marder A{{D}}+ version you can press left CTRL + T to turn on the thermal overlay.\r\n\r\n-The Milan missile is used if you press F to switch to it. Hold the left mouse button to fire, and keep the reticle centered on your target to kill it. The missile launcher has no nightsight.\r\n\r\n-Simple Marder A{{E}}+ ammunition description\r\nAPI-T is a slug that is good at killing APCs and IFVs\r\nHE is an explosive that is good for killing infantry and trucks.\r\nMilan is a missile that is good at killing tanks.\r\n=本任务旨在帮助您熟悉Marder A{{A}}+及其火控系统。总计有{{B}}个目标：4个固定目标和{{C}}个移动目标，均处于不同距离。\r\n\r\n-火控系统快速使用指南\r\n按Shift键进入炮手瞄准镜。按住鼠标右键移动火炮。按CTRL+鼠标滚轮（或PgUp和PgDown键）依据抬头显示器（HUD）上的距离数据进行测距。将瞄准镜中心对准目标，若目标正在移动，需进行提前量瞄准。向目标开火，并根据需要调整测距。重复以上步骤，消灭所有目标。\r\n\r\n在炮手瞄准镜视角下按T键使用夜视瞄准镜。然后按N键开启红外探照灯。在Marder A{{D}}+型号上，您可以按左CTRL+T键开启热成像。\r\n\r\n-按F键可切换至Milan导弹。按住鼠标左键发射，并保持瞄准线持续对准目标直至命中。导弹发射器无夜视功能。\r\n\r\n-Marder A{{E}}+弹药简介\r\nAPI-T是一种穿甲弹，擅于摧毁APC和IFV。\r\nHE是一种高爆弹，适用于消灭步兵和卡车。\r\nMilan是一种导弹，擅于摧毁坦克。\r\n

Situation - A weakened enemy battalion is attempting to retreat under the cover of night\n\n\nEnemy - Approximately company-sized force remaining with M{{A}}s, M{{B}}s, and supporting elements\n\n\nFriendly - T-{{C}} company, BMP platoon, howitzer battery with HE and limited smoke rounds, scout helicopter flight\n\n\nMission - Delay the enemy's withdrawal to allow our flanking force to destroy them at their rally point\n\n\nCoordinating Instructions - When you have reached the blocking position you will take command of the flanking force for the final attack\n\n\nOther - Artillery flare support will not be available in order to preserve the element of surprise\n\n\nEnd Conditions -\n-Red Victory- &gt;{{D}}% of enemy force destroyed\n-Red Defeat- Enemy consolidates and withdraws, pursuit force is immobilized, or flanking force is destroyed\n=态势 - 一支削弱的敌军营正在夜幕掩护下试图撤退\n\n\n敌军 - 剩余大约连级规模力量，包括M{{A}}，M{{B}}，以及支援分队\n\n\n友军 - T-{{C}}连队，BMP排，榴弹炮连配HE和有限烟幕弹，侦察直升机编队\n\n\n任务 - 延缓敌军撤退，以允许我方侧翼部队在其集结点摧毁他们\n\n\n协调指令 - 当您抵达阻击阵地时，您将指挥侧翼部队进行最终攻击\n\n\n其他 - 为了保持突然性，炮兵照明弹支援将不可用\n\n\n结束条件 -\n-红方胜利- &gt;{{D}}%敌军力量被摧毁\n-红方失败- 敌军集结并撤退，追击部队被瘫痪，或侧翼部队被摧毁

Situation: You stopped the enemy’s attack approximately {{A}} hours ago. The rest of your battalion has arrived and is planning a limited counter-attack. Our scouts have observed the enemy is in the process of digging in and an attack any later in the future would be more difficult. Friendly divisional artillery is conducting counter battery fire missions to suppress the enemy’s access to artillery for this attack.\r\n\r\nEnemy: Company Strength, {{B}}x T{{C}}A, {{D}}x BTR{{E}}, {{F}}x infantry squads. {{G}}x T{{H}} reserve force\r\n\r\nFriendly: {{I}}x Leo A{{J}}A{{K}}, {{L}}x M{{M}}G, {{N}}x Infantry squads. Reinforcements of {{O}}x Leo A{{P}}A{{Q}}. Support assets are {{R}}x fire missions,and {{S}}x WP Smoke missions.\r\n\r\nMission: With your {{T}} tanks, you engage enemy forces on OBJ Cougar in order to support {{U}}rd mech platoon’s dismounted infantry attack on it. Once OBJ Cougar is taken, {{V}}nd tank and {{W}}th mech will move up to support you. You will then move to engage any enemy forces on OBJ Ocelot to support {{X}}th platoon’s dismounted infantry attack on it.\r\n\r\nCoordinating instructions: Your infantry will attack once you have engaged the enemy on OBJ Cougar. Your infantry will attack OBJ Ocelot once you have destroyed the enemy armor on OBJ Ocelot.\r\n\r\nEnd Conditions-\r\n-Blue Victory- {{Y}}% Enemy Destroyed and Objectives Cougar &amp; Ocelot taken.\r\n-Blue Defeat- Loss of all player tanks\r\n=态势: 您在大约{{A}}小时前阻止了敌军攻击。营其余部队已抵达，正在计划有限反击。我方侦察兵观察到敌军正在挖掘工事，未来任何延迟攻击都将更困难。友军师级炮兵正在执行反炮兵火力任务，以压制敌军在本攻击中的炮兵使用。\r\n\r\n敌军: 连级规模，{{B}}辆T{{C}}A，{{D}}辆BTR{{E}}，{{F}}个步兵班。{{G}}辆T{{H}}预备力量\r\n\r\n友军: {{I}}辆Leo A{{J}}A{{K}}，{{L}}辆M{{M}}G，{{N}}个步兵班。增援部队{{O}}辆Leo A{{P}}A{{Q}}。支援资产包括{{R}}次火力任务，以及{{S}}次WP烟幕任务。\r\n\r\n任务: 使用您的{{T}}辆坦克，交战目标Cougar上的敌军力量，以支援{{U}}机械化排的下车步兵对其攻击。一旦目标Cougar被占领，{{V}}坦克和{{W}}机械化部队将上前支援您。然后您将移动交战目标Ocelot上的任何敌军力量，以支援{{X}}排的下车步兵对其攻击。\r\n\r\n协调指令: 一旦您交战目标Cougar上的敌军，您的步兵将发起攻击。一旦您摧毁目标Ocelot上的敌军装甲，您的步兵将攻击目标Ocelot。\r\n\r\n结束条件-\r\n-蓝方胜利- {{Y}}%敌军被摧毁且目标Cougar &amp; Ocelot被占领。\r\n-蓝方失败- 损失所有玩家坦克

Situation: A small enemy advance force has been detected occupying an important crossroads along the main supply route\r\n\r\nEnemy: Motor-rifle platoon(-)\r\n\r\nFriendly: {{A}}x Marder with mechanized infantry. Support assets are {{B}}x HE, and {{C}}x Smoke\r\n\r\nMission: Secure the crossroads, designated OBJ Romeo, for follow-on forces\r\n\r\nCoordinating Instructions: Two avenues of approach have been identified. Upon arriving at your chosen point, halt until the infantry have fully dismounted and then provide fire support as they advance on the objective\r\n\r\nEnd Conditions\r\nBlue Victory: OBJ Romeo secured\r\nBlue Defeat: All friendly vehicles or infantry neutralized\r=态势: 发现一支小规模敌军先遣部队占据主要补给线上的重要十字路口\r\n\r\n敌军: 摩托化步枪排(-)\r\n\r\n友军: {{A}}辆Marder配机械化步兵。支援资产包括{{B}}次HE，以及{{C}}次烟幕\r\n\r\n任务: 为后续部队夺取指定为目标Romeo的十字路口\r\n\r\n协调指令: 已确定两条进攻路线。到达您选择的位置后，停止前进直到步兵完全下车，然后在他们向目标推进时提供火力支援\r\n\r\n结束条件\r\n蓝方胜利: 目标Romeo被夺取\r\n蓝方失败: 所有友军载具或步兵被消灭

Showcase of all playable vehicles and WIP vehicles.\n\n\nThis map is work in progress and subject to change during development.=展示所有可玩车辆和半成品车辆。\n这张地图正在进行中，在开发过程中可能会发生变化。
This is a simple mission to get used to the M1 Abrams and its fire control. There will be 8 targets total: four of them static and 4 of them moving, all of them at different ranges.\n\n\n-A quick guide to using the fire control system\nPress Shift to enter the gunsight. Hold the right mouse button to move the gun. Center the gunsight on the target, and keep it centered if the target is moving. Press E to lase the target. Press the left mouse button to fire. This is important: if you have not already, release the right mouse button to re-center the gunsight. Repeat these steps to kill all targets.\n\n\n-If you press C when looking through your gunsight, it will switch to your backup sight.\n\n\nTo use the night sight press T when looking through the gunsight.\n\n\n-Simple M1 Abrams ammunition description\nAPFSDS is a dart that flies fast and is good at killing tanks.\nHEAT is an explosive that flies slow and is good for killing vehicles lighter than tanks, as well as old tanks.\n\n\nYou can see this briefing in the mission if you press M on your keyboard.\n=这是一个简单的任务，以适应M1 艾布拉姆斯及其火控。总共将有8个目标: 其中四个是静止的，四个是移动的，所有目标都在不同的距离。\n-使用火控系统的快速指南\n按Shift键使用火炮瞄具。按住鼠标右键移动火炮。将火炮瞄具对准目标，如果目标正在移动，则保持瞄具居中。按E键发射激光瞄准目标。按下鼠标左键开火。\n这一点很重要: 如果你没做到，请释放鼠标右键以重新调整瞄准镜的中心。重复这些步骤以击毁所有目标。\n-如果在瞄准镜视角按C键,它会切换到你的备用瞄准镜。\n要使用夜视仪，请在使用时瞄准镜按T。\n-简单的M1 艾布拉姆斯弹药描述\nAPFSDS是一种高弹速的钢针，擅长击毁坦克.\nHEAT是一种弹速较慢的炮弹，适用于击毁比坦克轻的车辆和旧坦克。\n如果你按下键盘上的M，你可以在任务中看到这个简报。\n
This is a simple mission to get used to the M60A3 TTS and its fire control. There will be 8 targets total: four of them static and 4 of them moving, all of them at different ranges.\n\n\n-A quick guide to using the fire control system\nPress Shift to enter the gunsight. Hold the right mouse button to move the gun. Center the gunsight on the target, and keep it centered if the target is moving. Press E to lase the target. Press the left mouse button to fire. This is important: if you have not already, release the right mouse button to re-center the gunsight. Repeat these steps to kill all targets.\n\n\n-If you press C when looking through your gunsight, it will switch to your backup sight.\n\n\nTo use the nightsight press T when looking through the gunsight.\n\n\n-Simple M60A3 TTS ammunition description\nAPFSDS is a dart that flies fast and is good at killing tanks.\nHEAT is an explosive that flies slow and is good for killing vehicles lighter than tanks, as well as old tanks.\n\n\nYou can see this briefing in the mission if you press M on your keyboard.\n=这是一个简单的任务，以适应M60A3 TTS及其火控。总共将有8个目标：其中四个是静止的，四个是移动的，所有目标都在不同的距离内。\n-使用火控系统的快速指南\n按Shift键使用火炮瞄具。按住鼠标右键移动火炮。将火炮瞄具对准目标，如果目标正在移动，则保持瞄具居中。按E键发射激光瞄准目标。按下鼠标左键开火。\n这一点很重要 : 如果你没做到，请释放鼠标右键以重新调整瞄准镜的中心。重复这些步骤以击毁所有目标。\n-如果在瞄准镜视角按C键,它会切换到你的备用瞄准镜。\n要使用夜视仪，请在使用时瞄准镜按T。\n-简单的M60A3 TTS弹药描述\nAPFSDS是一种高弹速的钢针，擅长击毁坦克。\nHEAT是一种弹速较慢的炮弹，适用于击毁比坦克轻的车辆和旧坦克。\n如果你按下键盘上的M，你可以在任务中看到这个简报。\n
This is a simple mission to get used to the M60A1 RISE(PASSIVE) and its fire control. There will be 8 targets total: four of them static and 4 of them moving, all of them at different ranges.\r\n\r\n-A quick guide to using the fire control system\r\nPress Shift to enter the gunsight. Hold the right mouse button to move the gun. Center the gunsight on the target. Press E to enable the coincidence rangefinder. Press CTRL+Mouse Wheel (or PgUp and PgDown) and adjust the range until the target’s double image merges into one. Try picking straight line features on the target to align. This will range your gun to the correct distance and you can now fire. Repeat these steps to kill all targets.\r\n\r\n-If you press C when looking through your gunsight, it will switch to your backup sight.\r\n-The night sight does not have the coincidence rangefinder.\r\n-The HUD shows your current range setting. Make sure you’re using a range that makes sense for the current target, especially if the coincidence rangefinder isn’t clear enough.\r\n\r\n-Simple M60A1 RISE(PASSIVE) ammunition description\r\nAPFSDS is a dart that flies fast and is good at killing tanks.\r\nHEAT is an explosive that flies slow and is good for killing vehicles lighter than tanks, as well as old tanks.\r\n\r\nYou can see this briefing in the mission if you press M on your keyboard.\r\n=这是一个简单的任务，以适应M60A1 RISE(PASSIVE)及其火控。总共将有8个目标: 其中四个是静止的，四个是移动的，所有目标都在不同的距离。\n-使用火控系统的快速指南\n按Shift键使用火炮瞄具。按住鼠标右键移动火炮。将火炮瞄具对准目标，按E启用合像测距仪.按CTRL+鼠标滚轮(或PgUp和PgDown)并调整射距直到目标的双图像合二为一。 \n尝试在目标上选择直线特征来对齐。这将使你的火炮射程达到正确的距离，你现在可以开火了。重复这些步骤以击毁所有目标。\n-如果在瞄准镜视角按C键,它会切换到你的备用瞄准镜。\n-夜视仪没有合像测距仪。\n-HUD显示你当前的密位设置。确保你使用的测距密位对当前目标有意义，尤其是在合像测距仪不够清晰的情况下。\n-简单的M60A1 RISE(PASSIVE)弹药描述\nAPFSDS是一种高弹速的钢针，擅长击毁坦克。\nHEAT是一种弹速较慢的炮弹，适用于击毁比坦克轻的车辆和旧坦克。\n如果你按下键盘上的M，你可以在任务中看到这个简报。\n
This is a simple mission to get used to the M2 Bradley and its fire control. There will be 8 targets total: four of them static and 4 of them moving, all of them at different ranges.\n\n\n-A quick guide to using the fire control system\nPress Shift to enter the gunsight. Hold the right mouse button to move the gun. Center the gunsight on the target. Press CTRL+Mouse Wheel (or PgUp and PgDown) to range the gun, and watch the range setting at the bottom of the sight unit. Fire on the target and adjust your range as needed. Repeat these steps to kill all targets.\n\n\nTo use the nightsight press T when looking through the gunsight.\n\n\n-The TOW missile is used if you press F to switch to it. Hold the left mouse button to fire, and keep the large crosshair centered on your target to kill it.\n\n\n-Simple M2 Bradley ammunition description\nAPDS is a dart that flies fast and is good at killing APCs and IFVs.\nHE is an explosive that flies slow and is good for killing infantry and trucks.\nBGM-71 TOW is a missile that is good at killing tanks.\n\n\nYou can see this briefing in the mission if you press M on your keyboard.\n=这是一个简单的任务，以适应M2 布莱德利及其火控。总共将有8个目标: 其中四个是静止的，四个是移动的，所有目标都在不同的距离。\n-使用火控系统的快速指南\n按Shift键使用机炮瞄具。按住鼠标右键移动机炮。将机炮瞄具对准目标。按下CTRL+鼠标滚轮(或PgUp和PgDown)来设置机炮射距，并观察瞄准镜底部的射距设置。向目标开火,并根据需要调整射距。重复这些步骤以击毁所有目标。\n要使用夜视仪，请在使用瞄准镜查看时按T。\n-当你按F键切换到TOW导弹，就可以使用它。按住鼠标左键开火，并将大十字准线对准目标以摧毁它。\n-简单的M2 布莱德利弹药描述\nAPDS是一种高弹速的钢针，擅长击毁APC和IFV。\nHE是一种弹速较慢的炮弹，适合杀伤步兵和击毁卡车。\nBGM-71 TOW是一种擅长击毁坦克的导弹。\n如果你按下键盘上的M，你可以在任务中看到这个简报。\n
This is a simple mission to get used to the T-72M and its fire control. There will be 8 targets total: four of them static and 4 of them moving, all of them at different ranges.\r\n\r\n-A quick guide to using the fire control system\r\nPress Shift to enter the gunsight. Hold the right mouse button to move the gun. Center the red circle on the target, and keep it centered if the target is moving. Press E to lase the target. The gunsight will adjust. Move the tip of the gunsight chevron to the target, and aim ahead of the target’s position if the target is moving. Press the left mouse button to fire. Repeat these steps to kill all targets.\r\n\r\n-The night sight does not have the laser rangefinder. To use it press T when looking through the gunsight. Then press N to turn on the IR searchlight. It is recommended to zero out the range when using the night sight.\r\n\r\n-Simple T-72M ammunition description\r\nAPFSDS is a dart that flies fast and is good at killing tanks.\r\nHEAT is an explosive that flies slow and is good for killing vehicles lighter than tanks, as well as old tanks.\r\nHE is an explosive that flies slow and is good for killing infantry and trucks.\r\n\r\nYou can see this briefing in the mission if you press M on your keyboard.\r\n=这是一个简单的任务，以适应T-72M及其火控。总共将有8个目标: 其中四个是静止的，四个是移动的，所有目标都在不同的距离。\n-使用火控系统的快速指南\n按Shift键使用火炮瞄具。按住鼠标右键移动火炮。将红色圆圈对准目标，如果目标正在移动，请将其保持居中。按E键发射激光瞄准目标。瞄准镜会自动调整。\n将火炮瞄具V形尖端移到目标处，如果目标正在移动，则瞄准目标位置的前方。按下鼠标左键开火，重复这些步骤以击毁所有目标。\n-夜视仪没有激光测距仪。要使用它，请在使用火炮瞄准镜时按T。然后按N打开红外探照灯。使用夜视仪时，建议将密位调零。\n-简单的T-72M弹药描述\nAPFSDS是一种高弹速的钢针，擅长击毁坦克。\nHEAT是一种弹速较慢的炮弹，适用于击毁比坦克轻的车辆和旧坦克。\nHE是一种弹速较慢的炮弹，适合杀伤步兵和击毁卡车。\n如果你按下键盘上的M，你可以在任务中看到这个简报。\n
This is a simple mission to get used to the T-55A and its fire control. There will be 8 targets total: four of them static and 4 of them moving, all of them at different ranges.\r\n\r\n-A quick guide to using the fire control system\r\nPress Shift to enter the gunsight. Hold the right mouse button to move the gun. Press CTRL+Mouse Wheel (or PgUp and PgDown) to range the gun using the range number in the HUD. Center the gunsight on the target, and aim ahead of the target’s position if the target is moving. Press the left mouse button to fire. Adjust the ranging if needed. Repeat these steps to kill all targets.\r\n\r\n-The night sight does not have the scrolling gunsight markings. To use it press T when looking through the gunsight. Then press N to turn on the IR searchlight. It is recommended to zero out the range when using the night sight.\r\n\r\n-Simple T-55A ammunition description\r\nAPFSDS is a dart that flies fast and is good at killing tanks.\r\nHEAT is an explosive that flies slow and is good for killing vehicles lighter than tanks, as well as old tanks.\r\nHE is an explosive that flies slow and is good for killing infantry and trucks.\r\nAPHE is an explosive that flies fast and is good for killing vehicles lighter than tanks, as well as old tanks.\r\n\r\nYou can see this briefing in the mission if you press M on your keyboard.\r\n=这是一个简单的任务，以适应T-55A及其火控。总共将有8个目标: 其中四个是静止的，四个是移动的，所有目标都在不同的距离。\n-使用火控系统的快速指南\n按Shift键使用火炮瞄具。按住鼠标右键移动火炮。按下CTRL+鼠标滚轮(或PgUp和PgDown)，来使用HUD中的射距数字设置火炮射距。\n将火炮瞄具对准目标，如果目标正在移动，则瞄准目标位置的前方。按下鼠标左键开火。如有必要，调整射距。重复这些步骤以击毁所有目标。\n-夜视仪没有滚动的瞄准镜标记。要使用它，请在使用火炮瞄准镜时按T。然后按N打开红外探照灯。使用夜视仪时，建议将射距调零。\n-简单的T-55A弹药描述\nAPFSDS是一种高弹速的钢针，擅长击毁坦克。\nHEAT是一种弹速较慢的炮弹，适用于击毁比坦克轻的车辆和旧坦克。\nHE是一种弹速较慢的炮弹，适合杀伤步兵和击毁卡车。\nAPHE是一种弹速较快的炮弹，适用于击毁比坦克轻的车辆和旧坦克。\n如果你按下键盘上的M，你可以在任务中看到这个简报。\n
This is a simple mission to get used to the BMP-2 and its fire control. There will be 8 targets total: four of them static and 4 of them moving, all of them at different ranges.\r\n\r\n-A quick guide to using the fire control system\r\nPress Shift to enter the gunsight. Hold the right mouse button to move the gun. Press CTRL+Mouse Wheel (or PgUp and PgDown) to range the gun using the range number in the HUD. Center the gunsight on the target, and aim ahead of the target’s position if the target is moving. Fire on the target and adjust your range as needed. Repeat these steps to kill all targets.\r\n\r\nTo use the nightsight press T when looking through the gunsight. Then press N to turn on the IR searchlight.\r\n\r\n-The Konkurs missile is used if you press F to switch to it. Hold the left mouse button to fire, and keep the reticle centered on your target to kill it. The missile launcher has no nightsight.\r\n\r\n-Simple BMP-2 ammunition description\r\nAPBC is a slug that is good at killing APCs and IFVs\r\nHEI is an explosive that is good for killing infantry and trucks.\r\n9M113 Konkurs is a missile that is good at killing tanks.\r\n\r\nYou can see this briefing in the mission if you press M on your keyboard.\r\n=这是一个简单的任务，以适应BMP-2及其火控。总共将有8个目标: 其中四个是静止的,四个是移动的,所有目标都在不同的距离。\n-使用火控系统的快速指南\n按Shift键使用机炮瞄具。按住鼠标右键移动机炮。按下CTRL+鼠标滚轮(或PgUp和PgDown)，来使用HUD中的密位数字设置机炮射程。\n将机炮瞄具对准目标，如果目标正在移动，则瞄准目标位置的前方。向目标开火，并根据需要调整射程。重复这些步骤以击毁所有目标。\n要使用夜视仪，请在使用瞄准镜时按T。然后按N打开红外探照灯。\n-当你按F键切换到竞赛导弹，就可以使用它。按住鼠标左键开火，并将十字线对准目标以摧毁它。导弹发射器没有夜视仪。\n-简单的BMP-2弹药描述\nAPBC是一种擅长击毁APC和IFV的穿甲弹\nHEI是一种爆炸弹，可以杀伤步兵和击毁卡车。\n9M113竞赛是一种擅长击毁坦克的导弹。\n如果你按下键盘上的M，你可以在任务中看到这个简报。\n
This is a simple mission to get used to the BMP-1 and its fire control. There will be 8 targets total: four of them static and 4 of them moving, all of them at different ranges.\r\n\r\n-A quick guide to using the fire control system\r\nPress Shift to enter the gunsight. Hold the right mouse button to move the gun. Center the gunsight on the target using the appropriate marking for the target’s distance. Fire on the target and adjust your range as needed. Repeat these steps to kill all targets.\r\n\r\n-The Malyutka missile is used if you press F to switch to it. Hold the left mouse button to fire. Steer the missile by pressing and holding the right mouse button. Attempt to keep the missile centered on the target. This is generally very difficult and requires practice. Large inputs may cause the missile to veer off course too far to recover in time.\r\n\r\nTo use the nightsight press T when looking through the gunsight. The IR searchlight is on the commander’s position and is not implemented yet.\r\n\r\n-Simple BMP-1 ammunition description\r\nHEAT is an explosive that flies slowly and is good for killing vehicles lighter than tanks, as well as old tanks.\r\nHE-Frag is an explosive that flies even more slowly and is good for killing infantry and trucks.\r\n9M14 Malyutka is a missile that is good at killing tanks.\r\n\r\nYou can see this briefing in the mission if you press M on your keyboard.\r\n=这是一个简单的任务，以适应BMP-1及其火控。总共将有8个目标: 其中四个是静止的，四个是移动的，所有目标都在不同的距离。\n-使用火控系统的快速指南\n按Shift键使用火炮瞄具。按住鼠标右键移动火炮。\n使用目标距离的适合标记,将火炮瞄具对准目标.向目标开火,并据需要调整距离.重复这些步骤以击毁所有目标。\n-当你按下F键切换到婴儿导弹，就可以使用它。按住鼠标左键开火。按住鼠标右键操纵导弹。\n尝试使导弹保持在目标的中心。这通常非常困难，需要练习。大量的输入可能会导致导弹偏离航向太远，无法及时恢复。\n要使用夜视仪，请在使用瞄准镜时按T。红外探照灯在车长的位置上，尚未生效。\n-简单的BMP-1弹药描述\nHEAT是一种弹速较慢的炮弹，适用于击毁比坦克轻的车辆和旧坦克。\nHE Frag是一种弹速更慢的炮弹,适合杀伤步兵和击毁卡车.\n9M14婴儿是一种擅长击毁坦克的导弹。\n如果你按下键盘上的M，你可以在任务中看到这个简报。\n
This is a simple mission to get used to the T-64A and its fire control. There will be 8 targets total: four of them static and 4 of them moving, all of them at different ranges.\r\n\r\n-A quick guide to using the fire control system\r\nPress Shift to enter the gunsight. Hold the right mouse button to move the gun. Center the gunsight on the target. Press E to enable the coincidence rangefinder. Press CTRL+Mouse Wheel (or PgUp and PgDown) and adjust the range until the target’s image is aligned in both the top and bottom image half circles. This will range your gun to the correct distance and you can now fire. Repeat these steps to kill all targets.\r\n\r\n\r\n-The night sight does not have the coincidence rangefinder. To use it press T when looking through the gunsight. Then press N to turn on the IR searchlight. It is recommended to zero out the range when using the night sight.\r\n\r\n-Simple T-64A ammunition description\r\nAPFSDS is a dart that flies fast and is good at killing tanks.\r\nHEAT is an explosive that flies slow and is good for killing vehicles lighter than tanks, as well as old tanks.\r\nHE is an explosive that flies slow and is good for killing infantry and trucks.\r\n\r\nYou can see this briefing in the mission if you press M on your keyboard.\r\n=这是一个简单的任务，以适应T-64A及其火控。总共将有8个目标: 其中四个是静止的，四个是移动的，所有目标都在不同的距离。\n-使用火控系统的快速指南\n按Shift键使用火炮瞄具。按住鼠标右键移动火炮。按住鼠标右键移动火炮。\n将火炮瞄具对准目标。按E启用合像测距仪。按CTRL+鼠标滚轮(或PgUp和PgDown)并调整射距，直到目标的图像在上方和下方图像的半圆中对齐。这将使你的火炮射距达到正确的距离，你现在可以开火了。重复这些步骤以击毁所有目标。\n-夜视仪没有合像测距仪。要使用它，请在火炮瞄准镜下按T。然后按N打开红外探照灯。使用夜视仪时，建议将密位调零。\n-简单的T-64A弹药描述\nAPFSDS是一种高弹速的钢针，擅长击毁坦克。\nHEAT是一种弹速较慢的炮弹，适用于击毁比坦克轻的车辆和旧坦克。\nHE是一种弹速较慢的炮弹，适合杀伤步兵和击毁卡车。\n如果你按下键盘上的M，你可以在任务中看到这个简报。\n

This is a simple mission to get used to the T-80B and its fire control. There will be 8 targets total: four of them static and 4 of them moving, all of them at different ranges.\n\n\n-A quick guide to using the fire control system\nPress Shift to enter the gunsight. Hold the right mouse button to move the gun. Center the tip of the gunsight chevron on the target, and keep it centered if the target is moving. Press E to lase the target. Press the left mouse button to fire. Repeat these steps to kill all targets.\n\n\n-The night sight does not use the laser rangefinder, if you use the laser when on the night sight it will not work and you will miss. To use the night sight press T when looking through the gunsight. Then press N to turn on the IR searchlight. Press CTRL+Mouse Wheel (or PgUp and PgDown) to range the gun using the range number in the HUD.\n\n\n-Simple T-80B ammunition description\nAPFSDS is a dart that flies fast and is good at killing tanks.\nHEAT is an explosive that flies slow and is good for killing vehicles lighter than tanks, as well as old tanks.\nHE is an explosive that flies slow and is good for killing infantry and trucks.\n\n\nYou can see this briefing in the mission if you press M on your keyboard.\n=这是一个简单的任务，以适应T-80B及其火控。总共将有8个目标: 其中四个是静止的，四个是移动的，所有目标都在不同的距离。\n-使用火控系统的快速指南\n按Shift键使用火炮瞄具。按住鼠标右键移动火炮。\n将火炮瞄准镜的V形尖端对准目标，如果目标正在移动，请将其保持居中。按E键发射激光瞄准目标。按下鼠标左键开火。重复这些步骤以击毁所有目标。\n-夜视仪没有激光测距仪，如果你在夜视仪视角下使用激光，它将不会生效，而你会脱靶。要使用夜视仪，请在使用瞄准镜时按T。然后按N打开红外探照灯。按下CTRL+鼠标滚轮(或PgUp和PgDown)，来使用HUD中的射距数字设置火炮射距。\n-简单的T-80B弹药描述\nAPFSDS是一种高弹速的钢针，擅长击毁坦克。\nHEAT是一种弹速较慢的炮弹，适用于击毁比坦克轻的车辆和旧坦克。\nHE是一种弹速较慢的炮弹，适合杀伤步兵和击毁卡车。\n如果你按下键盘上的M，你可以在任务中看到这个简报。\n
